VNB catalog: theorems, axioms, definitions, assertions

Auto-generated by (catalog). 2445 results — 928 proven, 576 support (PSS), 943 stated without proof (199 axioms, 608 definitions, 136 assertions).

> Trust. This catalog lists what is proven; how much each > proof still leans on unproved facts is the companion ledger > PROOF-DEBT.md. In short: a proof's bill > is the set of asserted facts it rests on; an empty bill prints > proven modulo 0 (unconditional relative to the kernel -- the > strongest report), and a non-empty bill carries a [trust: K] > tier equal to its weakest leaf's warrant, where none > (a leaf with no warrant at all) is weakest and proof strongest. > The ledger heads with the full glossary and tier table.

  • bernstein-weighted-step — forall([n_, x_, w_, p_, q_, s_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies forall([k_ in zz], w_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], q_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], s_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], p_(k_) = w_(k_) * (bernstein-basis(succ(n_), x_))(k_)) implies forall([k_ in nn], q_(k_) = w_(succ(k_)) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies forall([k_ in nn], s_(k_) = w_(k_) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(p_, succ(succ(n_))) = x_ * series-partial-sum(q_, succ(n_)) + (1 - x_) * series-partial-sum(s_, succ(n_)))
  • big-union-mono — forall([a_, b_], a_ subset b_ implies big-union(u_, a_, u_) subset big-union(u_, b_, u_))

  • rr-lt-add — forall([x, y, u, v], x in rr implies y in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies x < y and u <= v implies x + u < y + v)

  • rr-mono-le-limit — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= f(succ(k))) implies forall([lm_], converges-to(rr-ms, f, lm_) implies forall([m in nn], f(m) <= lm_)))

  • strictly-below-transitive — forall([grd, porel, x_, y_, z_], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies x_ in grd implies y_ in grd implies z_ in grd implies is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_) implies is-strictly-below(porel, y_, z_) implies is-strictly-below(porel, x_, z_))
  • sub-continuous-at — forall([g, h, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) - h(x)), a))
  • sub-lam-in-fun — forall([g, h], g in fun(rr, rr) implies h in fun(rr, rr) implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) - h(x)) in fun(rr, rr))
  • subclass-of-set-is-set — forall([a, b in set], a subset b implies a in set)
  • submodule-fg — forall([md], is-module(md) and is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], generates(md, n, u) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= n) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm))))))
  • submodule-intersection — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([s1], is-submodule(md, s1) implies forall([s2], is-submodule(md, s2) implies is-submodule(md, intersection(s1, s2)))))
  • submodule-nvs-module-view — forall([m_, t_], is-submodule(m_, t_) implies is-submodule(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_), t_))
  • subseq-of-convergent — forall([s, f, phi, l], converges-to(s, f, l) implies strictly-mono-nn(phi) implies converges-to(s, subseq(f, phi), l))
  • subsequence-principle — forall([s, f, l], is-metric-space(s) implies f in fun(nn, pts(s)) implies l in pts(s) implies forall([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) implies forsome([psi], strictly-mono-nn(psi) and converges-to(s, subseq(subseq(f, phi), psi), l))) implies converges-to(s, f, l))
  • subset-mem — forall([a, b, x_], a subset b implies x_ in a implies x_ in b)
  • subset-mem-fwd — forall([a, b, x], a subset b implies x in a implies x in b)
  • subset-trans — forall([a, b, c], a subset b implies b subset c implies a subset c)
  • subsqn-preserves-sqn — forall([a, f, y], f in sqn(a) implies subsqn(y, f) implies y in sqn(a))
  • sum-continuous-at — forall([g, h, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) + h(x)), a))
  • sum-expansion — forall([n in nn, r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r), g in fun(zz, carr(r))], sum(r, vnb-lambda(k, nn, (add(r))((mul(r))(x, g(k - 1)), (mul(r))(y, g(k)))), succ(n)) = (add(r))((add(r))((mul(r))((add(r))(x, y), sum(r, g, n)), (mul(r))(x, g(0 - 1))), (mul(r))(y, g(n)))))
  • sum-lam-in-fun — forall([g, h], g in fun(rr, rr) implies h in fun(rr, rr) implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) + h(x)) in fun(rr, rr))
  • supp-in-set — forall([a_, m_, f_], carr(m_) in set implies supp(a_, m_, f_) in set)
  • supp-membership — forall([a_, m_, f_, x_], x_ in supp(a_, m_, f_) iff x_ in carr(m_) and not(f_(x_) = zero(a_)))
  • supp-unfold — forall([a_, m_, f_], supp(a_, m_, f_) == {x_ in carr(m_): not(f_(x_) = zero(a_))})
  • swap-to-corner — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([m, n, a, i0, j0], a in mat(m, n, carr(r)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies i0 in interval(1, m) implies j0 in interval(1, n) implies not(1 = i0) implies not(1 = j0) implies forsome([b], mat-equiv(r, m, n, a, b) and entry(b, 1, 1) = entry(a, i0, j0))))
  • swap-to-corner-gen — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([m, n, a, i0, j0], a in mat(m, n, carr(r)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies i0 in interval(1, m) implies j0 in interval(1, n) implies forsome([b], mat-equiv(r, m, n, a, b) and entry(b, 1, 1) = entry(a, i0, j0))))
  • taylor-lagrange — forall([f, a, x, n], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and x in rr and n in nn and a < x implies taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < x and factorial(succ(n)) * (f(x) - taylor-poly(f, a, n, x)) = (nth-deriv(f, succ(n)))(theta) * (x - a) ^ succ(n)))
  • totally-bounded-has-cauchy-subsequence — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s))], forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and is-cauchy-seq(s, subseq(f, phi)))))
  • trinum-mono — forall([b_ in nn, a_ in nn], a_ <= b_ implies trinum(a_) <= trinum(b_))
  • trinum-type — forall([n_ in nn], trinum(n_) in nn)
  • triple-entry-left — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies entry(matmul(a, matmul(a, p, q), r), row, col) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, n))), interval(1, k))))
  • triple-entry-right — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies entry(matmul(a, p, matmul(a, q, r)), row, col) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))), interval(1, n))))
  • trunc-metric-bounded — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies (dist(trunc-metric(s)))(u_, v_) <= 1))
  • trunc-metric-dist — forall([s, u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies (dist(trunc-metric(s)))(u_, v_) == min(1, (dist(s))(u_, v_)))
  • trunc-metric-id-uniformly-continuous — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-uniformly-continuous(s, trunc-metric(s), vnb-lambda(a_, pts(s), a_)))
  • trunc-metric-id-uniformly-continuous-back — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-uniformly-continuous(trunc-metric(s), s, vnb-lambda(a_, pts(s), a_)))
  • trunc-metric-is-metric-space — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(trunc-metric(s)))
  • trunc-metric@preimage — forall([s, f, v], preimage(trunc-metric(s), f, v) == preimage(s, f, v))
  • tuples-induction — forall([a, c], [] in c and forall([x, m], x in a and m in tuples(a) and m in c implies cons(x, m) in c) implies forall([l in tuples(a)], l in c))
  • unif-cauchy-cont-implies-uniform-limit — forall([s, fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr))], forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies is-unif-cauchy(s, fam) implies forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g) and converges-uniformly(s, fam, g)))
  • uniform-cauchy-limit — forall([g in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), s_], forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn, l_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies n_ <= l_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in s_ implies abs((g(k_))(x_) - (g(l_))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies forsome([h in fun(rr, rr)], forall([x_ in rr], x_ in s_ implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (g(k_))(x_)), h(x_))) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in s_ implies abs(h(x_) - (g(k_))(x_)) <= eps))))))
  • uniform-limit-continuous — forall([s, fam, g], fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies g in fun(pts(s), rr) implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies converges-uniformly(s, fam, g) implies is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g))
  • uniform-limit-continuous-at — forall([fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), g in fun(rr, rr), t_ in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, fam(k), t_)) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cp_ in nn], forall([k in nn], cp_ <= k implies forall([x_ in rr], abs(x_ - t_) <= rho implies abs(g(x_) - (fam(k))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, t_))
  • uniform-witness-diff-at — forall([gfam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), wfam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), fc in fun(rr, rr), lv in rr, a in rr, b in rr, t_ in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies forall([y_ in rr], abs(y_ - t_) <= rho implies y_ in ccint(a, b)) implies t_ in ccint(a, b) implies forall([k_ in nn], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, wfam(k_), t_)) implies forall([k_ in nn, y_ in rr], y_ in ccint(a, b) implies (gfam(k_))(y_) - (gfam(k_))(t_) = (wfam(k_))(y_) * (y_ - t_)) implies forall([y_ in rr], y_ in ccint(a, b) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (gfam(k_))(y_)), fc(y_))) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn, l_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies n_ <= l_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs((wfam(k_))(x_) - (wfam(l_))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (wfam(k_))(t_)), lv) implies is-diff-at(fc, t_, lv))
  • union-as-disjoint — forall([a_, b_], union(a_, b_) = union(a_, complement-in(b_, a_)))
  • union-assoc — forall([a_, b_, c_], union(union(a_, b_), c_) = union(a_, union(b_, c_)))
  • union-comm — forall([a_, b_], union(a_, b_) = union(b_, a_))
  • union-complement-in — forall([a_, b_], a_ subset b_ implies b_ = union(a_, complement-in(b_, a_)))
  • union-empty-right — forall([a_], union(a_, empty-set) = a_)
  • union-right-subset — forall([a_, s_], s_ subset union(a_, s_))
  • union-singleton-absorb — forall([s_, y_ in s_], union(pair(y_, y_), s_) = s_)
  • union-singleton-mem — forall([y_, s_], y_ in set implies y_ in union(pair(y_, y_), s_))
  • union-singleton-subset — forall([b_, y_, s_], y_ in b_ implies s_ subset b_ implies union(pair(y_, y_), s_) subset b_)
  • vector-taylor-remainder-bound — forall([m, f, a, x, n], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(m)) and f in fun(rr, vec(m)) and a in rr and x in rr and n in nn and a < x implies taylor-differentiable-v(m, f, a, x, n) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < x and factorial(succ(n)) * (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(f(x), (vneg(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n)))) <= (vnrm(m))((nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(theta)) * (x - a) ^ succ(n)))
  • whole-module-is-submodule — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-submodule(md, vec(md)))
  • zen-exhausts — forall([s in set], forsome([alpha in ord], not(forsome([x], x in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))}))))
  • zen-hits — forall([alpha in ord, s], forsome([x], x in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))}) implies zen(s, alpha) in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))})
  • zen-step — forall([alpha in ord, x], zen(x, alpha) = choice({y_ in x: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))
  • zero-is-antiderivable — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, 0), a, b))
  • zero-seq-in-fun — vnb-lambda(n_, nn, 0) in fun(nn, rr)
  • zero-series-converges — series-converges(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, 0))
  • zorn-chain-plus-keepset — forall([phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies is-chain(grd, porel, kset) implies is-chain(grd, porel, union(kset, keep-set(phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha))))
  • zorn-empty-is-chain — forall([grd, porel], grd in set implies is-chain(grd, porel, empty-set))
  • zorn-lemma — forall([grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies forsome([w_], w_ in grd) implies forall([ch_], is-chain(grd, porel, ch_) implies forsome([u_], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch_, u_))) implies forsome([mx_], is-maximal(grd, porel, mx_)))
  • zorn-zkept-is-chain — forall([alpha in ord, phi, grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies is-chain(grd, porel, zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha)))
  • zorn-zkept-limit-is-chain — forall([phi, grd, porel, lam], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies limit-ord(lam) implies forall([beta in ord-segment(lam)], is-chain(grd, porel, zkept(phi, grd, porel, beta))) implies is-chain(grd, porel, big-union(beta2, ord-segment(lam), zkept(phi, grd, porel, beta2))))
  • zorn-zkept-monotone — forall([b in ord, phi, grd, porel, a in ord], ord-le(a, b) implies zkept(phi, grd, porel, a) subset zkept(phi, grd, porel, b))
  • zup-injective — forall([grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) and forsome([w_], w_ in grd) and forall([ch_], is-chain(grd, porel, ch_) implies forsome([u_], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch_, u_))) and forall([x_ in grd], forsome([y_ in grd], is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_))) implies forall([a1_, a2_], a1_ in ord implies a2_ in ord implies zup(grd, porel, a1_) = zup(grd, porel, a2_) implies a1_ = a2_))
  • zup-tower — forall([a_ in ord, grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) and forsome([w_], w_ in grd) and forall([ch_], is-chain(grd, porel, ch_) implies forsome([u_], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch_, u_))) and forall([x_ in grd], forsome([y_ in grd], is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_))) implies zup(grd, porel, a_) in grd and forall([b_], ord-lt(b_, a_) implies zup(grd, porel, b_) in grd and is-strictly-below(porel, zup(grd, porel, b_), zup(grd, porel, a_))))
  • zz-add-cancel — forall([a in zz, b in zz, c in zz], a + c = b + c implies a = b)
  • zz-bezout — forall([a, b], a in zz implies b in zz implies forsome([d in zz-bezout-set(a, b)], zz-divides(d, a) and zz-divides(d, b)))
  • zz-bezout-set-is-ideal — forall([a, b], a in zz implies b in zz implies is-ideal(zz-ring, zz-bezout-set(a, b)))
  • zz-in-rr — forall([k_ in zz], k_ in rr)
  • zz-is-commutative-ring — is-commutative-ring(zz-ring)
  • zz-is-integral-domain — is-integral-domain(zz-ring)
  • zz-is-ring — is-ring(zz-ring)
  • zz-mul-cancel-zero — forall([a in zz, b in zz], a * b = 0 implies not(b = 0) implies a = 0)
  • zz-no-zero-divisors — forall([a in zz, b in zz], a * b = 0 implies a = 0 or b = 0)
  • zz-sub-in-zz — forall([u_, v_], u_ in zz implies v_ in zz implies u_ - v_ in zz)

Proof Support Set

Results we accept without a machine proof in VNB. Logically treated as theorems.

  • abelian-group-assoc — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c)))) [warrant: well-known]
  • abelian-group-inverse-unique — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) = iden(s) implies b = (inv(s))(a))) [warrant: well-known]
  • abelian-group-right-id — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, iden(s)) = a)) [warrant: well-known]
  • abs-nonneg-le — forall([a, c], a in rr implies 0 <= a implies abs(a) <= c implies a <= c) [warrant: well-known]
  • absolute-summable-implies-summable — forall([grp], is-normed-ag(grp) and is-complete(nag-metric-space(grp)) implies forall([f], f in fun(dom(f), carr(grp)) and is-absolutely-summable(grp, f) implies is-summable(grp, f))) [warrant: informal]
  • amgm-2-sqrt — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies forall([b], b in rr and 0 <= b implies sqrt(a * b) <= (/)(a + b, 2))) [warrant: well-known]
  • ascoli-arzela-sequential — forall([s, fam], is-compact(s) implies fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies is-equicontinuous(s, rr-ms, fam) implies pointwise-bounded(s, fam) implies forsome([del], strictly-mono-nn(del) and forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g) and converges-uniformly(s, subseq(fam, del), g)))) [warrant: reference]
  • baire-category — forall([s], is-complete(s) implies is-nonmeager(s, pts(s))) [warrant: reference]
  • ball-2r-triangle — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies forall([y in ball(s, x, r), z in ball(s, x, r)], (dist(s))(y, z) <= r + r and not((dist(s))(y, z) = r + r)))) [warrant: proof]
  • ball-center-in — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies x in ball(s, x, r))) [warrant: proof]
  • ball-cover-is-open-cover — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies is-open-cover(s, ball-cover(s, r)))) [warrant: well-known]
  • ball-cover-mem-fwd — forall([s, r, u in ball-cover(s, r)], forsome([c in pts(s)], ball(s, c, r) = u)) [warrant: well-known]
  • ball-is-open — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies is-open(s, ball(s, x, r)))) [warrant: proof]
  • ball-is-set — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x, r], ball(s, x, r) in set)) [warrant: proof]
  • ball-mem-from-le — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x, y, d, r], y in pts(s) and d in rr and r in rr and (dist(s))(x, y) <= d and d < r implies y in ball(s, x, r))) [warrant: proof]
  • ball-membership — forall([s, x, r, y], y in ball(s, x, r) iff y in pts(s) and (dist(s))(x, y) <= r and not((dist(s))(x, y) = r)) [warrant: proof]
  • ball-point-le — forall([s, c, r, w, p in w], ball(s, c, r) = w implies (dist(s))(c, p) <= r) [warrant: well-known]
  • ball-point-ne — forall([s, c, r, w, p in w], ball(s, c, r) = w implies not((dist(s))(c, p) = r)) [warrant: well-known]
  • bdd-fn-le-arg — forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies (/)(t, 1 + t) <= t) [warrant: well-known]
  • bdd-fn-lt-one — forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies (/)(t, 1 + t) < 1) [warrant: well-known]
  • bdd-fn-mono — forall([s in rr, t in rr], 0 <= s and s <= t implies (/)(s, 1 + s) <= (/)(t, 1 + t)) [warrant: well-known]
  • bdd-fn-nonneg — forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies 0 <= (/)(t, 1 + t)) [warrant: well-known]
  • bdd-fn-subadd — forall([a in rr, b in rr], 0 <= a and 0 <= b implies (/)(a + b, 1 + (a + b)) <= (/)(a, 1 + a) + (/)(b, 1 + b)) [warrant: well-known]
  • bdd-linfun-abs-real — forall([m, f, x], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f) implies x in vec(m) implies abs(f(x)) in rr) [warrant: reference]
  • bdd-metric-bounded — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x, y) < 1)) [warrant: well-known]
  • bdd-metric-carrier — forall([s], pts(bdd-metric(s)) == pts(s)) [warrant: well-known]
  • bdd-metric-distance — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x, y) = (/)((dist(s))(x, y), 1 + (dist(s))(x, y)))) [warrant: well-known]
  • bdd-metric-id-bicontinuous — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-continuous(s, bdd-metric(s), vnb-lambda(x, pts(s), x)) and is-continuous(bdd-metric(s), s, vnb-lambda(x, pts(bdd-metric(s)), x))) [warrant: well-known]

  • succ-not-one — forall([q, z in interval(1, q)], not(succ(z) = 1)) [warrant: well-known]
  • sum-ag-permutation-invariance — forall([n in nn, ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([g in fun(nn, carr(ag)), h in fun(nn, carr(ag)), phi in bijection(ord-segment(n), ord-segment(n))], forall([i in ord-segment(n)], h(i) = g(phi(i))) implies sum-ag(ag, g, n) = sum-ag(ag, h, n))) [warrant: informal]
  • sum-set-left-scalar — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies forall([f in fun(x, carr(s))], (mul(s))(a, sum-set(s, x, f)) = sum-set(s, x, vnb-lambda(z, x, (mul(s))(a, f(z))))))) [warrant: informal]
  • sum-set-right-scalar — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([b in carr(s), x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies forall([f in fun(x, carr(s))], (mul(s))(sum-set(s, x, f), b) = sum-set(s, x, vnb-lambda(z, x, (mul(s))(f(z), b)))))) [warrant: informal]
  • summable-bound-converges — forall([s, f, rad], is-complete(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and rad in fun(nn, rr) and series-converges(rad) and forall([k in nn], (dist(s))(f(k), f(succ(k))) <= rad(k)) implies converges(s, f)) [warrant: well-known]
  • summable-bound-implies-cauchy — forall([s, f, rad], is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and rad in fun(nn, rr) and series-converges(rad) and forall([k in nn], (dist(s))(f(k), f(succ(k))) <= rad(k)) implies is-cauchy-seq(s, f)) [warrant: well-known]
  • taylor-clear — forall([fn1 in rr, pw in rr, d in rr, r in rr, gx, ga, hx, ha, n in nn], 0 < pw implies gx = 0 implies hx = 0 implies ha = d implies ga = r implies (0 - (recip(factorial(n)) * fn1) * pw) * (hx - ha) = (0 - succ(n) * pw) * (gx - ga) implies factorial(succ(n)) * r = fn1 * d) [warrant: well-known]
  • taylor-deriv-real — forall([f, a, x, n, t], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies a < t implies t < x implies (nth-deriv(f, succ(n)))(t) in rr) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-g-at-a — forall([f, a, x, n], (vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)))(a) = f(x) - taylor-poly(f, a, n, x)) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-g-at-x — forall([f, x, n in nn], (vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)))(x) = 0) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-g-cont — forall([f, a, x, n, t], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies t in ccint(a, x) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)), t)) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-g-diff — forall([f, a, x, n, t], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies a < t and t < x implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)), t, 0 - (recip(factorial(n)) * (nth-deriv(f, succ(n)))(t)) * (x - t) ^ n)) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-g-in-fun — forall([n in nn, f in fun(rr, rr), x in rr], vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)) in fun(rr, rr)) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-gmvt-cont — forall([f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies x in rr implies forall([t in ccint(a, x)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)), t) and is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)), t))) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-gmvt-diff — forall([f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies forall([t], t in rr and a < t and t < x implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)), t, l)) and forsome([m], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)), t, m)))) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-h-at-a — forall([a, x, n], (vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)))(a) = (x - a) ^ succ(n)) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-h-at-x — forall([x, n in nn], (vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)))(x) = 0) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-h-cont — forall([x, n, t], x in rr implies t in rr implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)), t)) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-h-diff — forall([x, n, t], x in rr implies t in rr implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)), t, 0 - succ(n) * (x - t) ^ n)) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-h-in-fun — forall([n in nn, x in rr], vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)) in fun(rr, rr)) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-poly-at-center — forall([f, x, n], x in rr implies n in nn implies forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies (nth-deriv(f, k))(x) in rr) implies taylor-poly(f, x, n, x) = f(x)) [warrant: reference]
  • taylor-poly-in-rr — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, n in nn, x in rr], taylor-poly(f, a, n, x) in rr) [warrant: reference]
  • tb-block-step — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), r], pos-rr(r) implies forall([j in inf-subsets(nn)], forsome([j_ in inf-subsets(nn)], j_ subset j and forsome([c in pts(s)], forall([i in j_], f(i) in ball(s, c, r))))))) [warrant: reference]
  • tb-has-eps-cauchy-subseq — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and is-eps-cauchy-seq(s, eps, subseq(f, phi))))) [warrant: reference]
  • tb-rad-ball-cover — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([rad], forall([k in nn], pos-rr(rad(k))) implies pts(s) in set and forsome([cov], forall([k in nn], is-finite-cover(cov(k), pts(s))) and forall([k in nn, u in cov(k)], forsome([c in pts(s)], u = ball(s, c, rad(k))))))) [warrant: reference]
  • tb-scale-dense-seq — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([x0 in pts(s), r], pos-rr(r) implies forsome([g in fun(nn, pts(s))], forall([p in pts(s)], forsome([j in nn], (dist(s))(p, g(j)) < r))))) [warrant: well-known]
  • tel-dist-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, k) implies vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, n), (mul(a))((vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(a))(entry(p, row, j), entry(q, j, x))))(z), entry(r, x, col))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a))))) [warrant: well-known]
  • tel-inf-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, k) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(a))(entry(p, row, j), entry(q, j, x))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(a)))) [warrant: well-known]
  • tel-outf-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, k), (mul(a))(entry(matmul(a, p, q), row, j), entry(r, j, col))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(ring-additive-ag(a))))) [warrant: well-known]
  • tel-red-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, k) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, j), entry(q, j, x)), entry(r, x, col))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a))))) [warrant: well-known]
  • tel-tout-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, n))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(ring-additive-ag(a))))) [warrant: well-known]
  • ter-dist-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, n) implies vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, k), (mul(a))(entry(p, row, x), (vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, k), (mul(a))(entry(q, x, j), entry(r, j, col))))(z))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(ring-additive-ag(a))))) [warrant: well-known]
  • ter-gj-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, n) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, k), (mul(a))(entry(q, x, j), entry(r, j, col))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(a)))) [warrant: well-known]
  • ter-outf-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(a))(entry(p, row, j), entry(matmul(a, q, r), j, col))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a))))) [warrant: well-known]
  • ter-red-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, n) implies vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, x), entry(q, x, c)), entry(r, c, col))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(ring-additive-ag(a))))) [warrant: well-known]
  • ter-tout-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a))))) [warrant: well-known]
  • tuple-cons-decompose — forall([a, n, l], n in nn and l in tuples(a) and length(l) = succ(n) implies forsome([x in a, m in tuples(a)], length(m) = n and l = cons(x, m))) [warrant: well-known]
  • tuple-length-zero — forall([a, l], l in tuples(a) and length(l) = 0 implies l = []) [warrant: well-known]
  • unif-cauchy-has-uniform-limit — forall([s, fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr))], is-unif-cauchy(s, fam) implies forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], converges-uniformly(s, fam, g))) [warrant: reference]
  • uniformly-continuous-is-continuous — forall([s, t, f], is-uniformly-continuous(s, t, f) implies is-continuous(s, t, f)) [warrant: informal]
  • union-empty-left — forall([a in set], union(empty-set, a) = a) [warrant: informal]
  • union-of-opens-open — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a, g], forall([i in a], is-open(s, g(i))) implies is-open(s, big-union(i, a, g(i))))) [warrant: proof]
  • unitrow-entry-at — forall([a, n, i in interval(1, n)], entry(unitrow(a, n, i), 1, i) = one(a)) [warrant: reference]
  • unitrow-entry-off — forall([a, n, i, j], i in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(j = i) implies entry(unitrow(a, n, i), 1, j) = zero(a)) [warrant: reference]
  • unitrow-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, i], unitrow(a, n, i) in mat(1, n, carr(a)))) [warrant: reference]
  • vec-is-set — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies vec(m) in set) [warrant: reference]
  • vnrm-nonneg — forall([m, w_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies w_ in vec(m) implies 0 <= (vnrm(m))(w_)) [warrant: reference]
  • vnrm-real — forall([m, w_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies w_ in vec(m) implies (vnrm(m))(w_) in rr) [warrant: reference]
  • vspace-vec-is-set — forall([m], is-vector-space(m) implies vec(m) in set) [warrant: reference]
  • vtaylor-clear — forall([rr in rr, gv in rr, nv in rr, pw in rr, n in nn], factorial(succ(n)) * rr = gv * pw implies gv <= nv implies 0 <= pw implies factorial(succ(n)) * rr <= nv * pw) [warrant: well-known]
  • vtaylor-poly-in-vec — forall([m, f, a, x, n], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies a in rr implies x in rr implies n in nn implies taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n) in vec(m)) [warrant: reference]
  • vtaylor-remainder-in-vec — forall([m, f, a, x, n], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies a in rr implies x in rr implies n in nn implies (vadd(m))(f(x), (vneg(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n))) in vec(m)) [warrant: reference]
  • well-ordering-principle — forall([s in set], forsome([phi], phi in bijection(ord-segment(card(s)), s))) [warrant: well-known]
  • young-inequality — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies forall([b], b in rr and 0 < b implies forall([p], p in qq and 1 < p implies forall([q], q in qq and 1 < q implies (/)(1, p) + (/)(1, q) = 1 implies a * b <= (/)(rpow(a, p), p) + (/)(rpow(b, q), q))))) [warrant: well-known]
  • zeromat-type — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n], zeromat(a, m, n) in mat(m, n, carr(a)))) [warrant: reference]

Stated without proof

Not proved in VNB; split by origin. "Axiom" is reserved for the first group — it is NOT the umbrella for this section.

Axioms — the fixed VNB base

The foundational axioms of VNB set theory (make-vnb-base-theory core + theorem-library/axioms). Fixed, though not finite (separation/replacement are schemas). Everything else rests on these.

> The axioms below are NOT the whole trusted base. The set- & class-formation schemas (separation {x∈A|p}, comprehension {x|p}, indexed union ⋃_{z∈A}body) carry a formula schema in their body and so live in the proof checker as primitive inference rules, not as formulas in this table. They are documented in KERNEL-RULES.md, which is the honest statement of the trusted base: these axioms plus the kernel rules.

  • app-graph — forall([fn_, arg_], fn_(arg_) == iota(val_, [arg_, val_] in fn_))
  • apply-tupling-1 — forall([f, a], f(a) == f([a]))
  • apply-tupling-2 — forall([f, a, b], f(a, b) == f([a, b]))
  • apply-tupling-3 — forall([f, a, b, c], f(a, b, c) == f([a, b, c]))
  • burali-forti — not(ord in set)
  • card-empty — card(empty-set) = 0
  • card-finite-bij — forall([a], a in set and card(a) in nn implies forsome([phi], phi in bijection(ord-segment(card(a)), a)))
  • card-image-injection — forall([dm, cod, phi], phi in injection(dm, cod) and dm in set and card(dm) in nn implies card(image(phi, dm)) = card(dm))
  • card-in-ord — forall([a in set], card(a) in ord)
  • card-insert — forall([a in set, x], x in set and not(x in a) implies card(union(a, pair(x, x))) = succ_ord(card(a)))
  • card-segment — forall([n in nn], card(ord-segment(n)) = n)
  • card-union-disjoint — forall([a], a in set and card(a) in nn implies forall([b], b in set and card(b) in nn and intersection(a, b) = empty-set implies card(union(a, b)) = card(a) + card(b)))
  • cartesian-set-iff — forall([a, b], cartesian(a, b) in set iff a in set and b in set)
  • cc-add-assoc — forall([a, b, c], a in cc and b in cc and c in cc implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
  • cc-add-closed — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a + b in cc)
  • cc-add-comm — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a + b = b + a)
  • cc-add-zero — forall([a in cc], a + 0 = a)
  • cc-conjugate-add — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies conjugate(a + b) = conjugate(a) + conjugate(b))
  • cc-conjugate-closed — forall([a in cc], conjugate(a) in cc)
  • cc-conjugate-fixes-rr — forall([a in rr], conjugate(a) = a) [warrant: reference]
  • cc-conjugate-i — conjugate(1i) = -1i [warrant: reference]
  • cc-conjugate-involution — forall([a in cc], conjugate(conjugate(a)) = a)
  • cc-conjugate-mul — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies conjugate(a * b) = conjugate(a) * conjugate(b))
  • cc-distributive — forall([a, b, c], a in cc and b in cc and c in cc implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
  • cc-generated-by-rr — forall([z in cc], forsome([x in rr, y in rr], z = x + y * 1i)) [warrant: reference]
  • cc-i-in — 1i in cc [warrant: reference]
  • cc-i-squared — 1i * 1i = -1 [warrant: reference]
  • cc-is-set — cc in set
  • cc-mul-assoc — forall([a, b, c], a in cc and b in cc and c in cc implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
  • cc-mul-closed — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a * b in cc)
  • cc-mul-comm — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a * b = b * a)
  • cc-neg-closed — forall([a in cc], -a in cc)
  • cc-neg-inverse — forall([a in cc], a + -a = 0)
  • cc-one-in — 1 in cc
  • cc-one-mul — forall([a in cc], 1 * a = a)
  • cc-recip-closed — forall([a], a in cc and not(a = 0) implies recip(a) in cc)
  • cc-recip-inverse — forall([a], a in cc and not(a = 0) implies a * recip(a) = 1)
  • cc-self-conj-nonneg — forall([a in cc], a * conjugate(a) in rr and 0 <= a * conjugate(a))
  • cc-self-conj-real — forall([a in cc], a * conjugate(a) in rr)
  • cc-zero-in — 0 in cc
  • choice-axiom — forall([a], forsome([x], x in a) implies choice(a) in a)
  • class-extensionality — forall([a, b], forall([x], x in a iff x in b) implies a = b)
  • complement-in-membership — forall([a, b, x], x in complement-in(a, b) iff x in a and not(x in b))
  • complement-in-set-closure — forall([a, b], a in set implies complement-in(a, b) in set)
  • dom-fun-membership — forall([a, f in fun(a), x], x in dom(f) iff x in a)
  • dom-membership — forall([f, x], x in dom(f) iff x in set and f(x) = f(x))
  • dom-of-fun — forall([a, f], f in fun(a) and a in set implies dom(f) in set and dom(f) = a)
  • empty-set-has-no-members — forall([x], not(x in empty-set))
  • empty-set-is-set — empty-set in set
  • eq-subst-membership — forall([a, b, s], a = b and b in s implies a in s)
  • equality-symmetry — forall([a, b], a = b implies b = a)
  • equality-transitivity — forall([a, b, c], a = b and b = c implies a = c)
  • extensionality — forall([a, b], a in set and b in set implies a = b iff forall([x], x in a iff x in b))
  • finite-set-induction — forall([c], empty-set in c and forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn and s in c implies forall([x], x in set and not(x in s) implies union(s, pair(x, x)) in c)) implies forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn implies s in c))
  • fun-apply-type — forall([f, a, b, x], f in fun(a, b) and x in a implies f(x) in b)
  • fun-codomain-iff — forall([a, b, f], f in fun(a, b) iff f in fun(a) and forall([x in a], f(x) in b))
  • fun-domain-apply-def — forall([a, f, x], f in fun(a) implies f(x) = f(x) iff x in a)
  • fun-domain-elements-are-sets — forall([a, f], f in fun(a) and a in set implies f in set)
  • fun-domain-extensionality — forall([a, f, g], f in fun(a) implies g in fun(a) implies forall([x in a], f(x) = g(x)) implies f = g)
  • fun-elements-are-sets — forall([a, b, f], f in fun(a, b) and a in set implies f in set)
  • fun-no-junk — forall([a, f in fun(a), z], z in f iff forsome([x, y], z = [x, y] and x in a and f(x) = y))
  • fun-set-iff — forall([a, b], fun(a, b) in set iff a in set and b in set)
  • image-set — forall([phi, s in set], image(phi, s) in set)
  • intersection-decompose — forall([x, as], x in intersection(restvar(as)) iff splice(and, e, as, x in e))
  • intersection-membership — forall([a, b, x], x in intersection(a, b) iff x in a and x in b)
  • intersection-set-closure — forall([a, b], a in set or b in set implies intersection(a, b) in set)
  • is-fun-def — forall([f], is-fun(f) iff forsome([a in set], f in fun(a)))
  • length-in-nn — forall([a, l in tuples(a)], length(l) in nn)
  • length-of-empty — length([]) = 0
  • limit-ord-is-sup — forall([lambda], limit-ord(lambda) implies sup-ord(ord-segment(lambda)) = lambda)
  • list-sethood — forall([a, l], a in set and l in tuples(a) implies l in set)
  • make-set-empty — {} = empty-set
  • make-set-membership — forall([x, l], x in make-set(l) iff x in set and forsome([i in nn], 1 <= i and i <= length(l) and nth(i, l) = x))
  • make-set-sethood — forall([a, l], l in tuples(a) and a in set implies make-set(l) in set)
  • membership-implies-sethood — forall([a, b], a in b implies a in set)
  • nn-add-assoc — forall([a, b, c], a in nn and b in nn and c in nn implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
  • nn-add-closed — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a + b in nn)
  • nn-add-comm — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a + b = b + a)
  • nn-add-zero — forall([a in nn], a + 0 = a)
  • nn-distributive — forall([a, b, c], a in nn and b in nn and c in nn implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
  • nn-induction — forall([c], 0 in c and forall([n], n in nn and n in c implies succ(n) in c) implies forall([n in nn], n in c))
  • nn-is-set — nn in set
  • nn-mul-assoc — forall([a, b, c], a in nn and b in nn and c in nn implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
  • nn-mul-closed — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a * b in nn)
  • nn-mul-comm — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a * b = b * a)
  • nn-one-mul — forall([a in nn], 1 * a = a)
  • nn-subset-ord — forall([n in nn], n in ord)
  • nn-subset-zz — forall([n in nn], n in zz)
  • nn-succ-closed — forall([n in nn], succ(n) in nn)
  • nn-zero-in — 0 in nn
  • nth-in-range — forall([a, i, l], i in nn and l in tuples(a) and 1 <= i and i <= length(l) implies nth(i, l) in a)
  • ord-le-antisymm — forall([alpha, beta], ord-le(alpha, beta) and ord-le(beta, alpha) implies alpha = beta)
  • ord-le-closure — forall([alpha, beta], ord-le(alpha, beta) implies alpha in ord and beta in ord)
  • ord-le-nn-compat — forall([m, n], m in nn and n in nn implies ord-le(m, n) iff m <= n)
  • ord-le-refl — forall([alpha in ord], ord-le(alpha, alpha))
  • ord-le-total — forall([alpha, beta], alpha in ord and beta in ord implies ord-le(alpha, beta) or ord-le(beta, alpha))
  • ord-le-trans — forall([alpha, beta, gamma], ord-le(alpha, beta) and ord-le(beta, gamma) implies ord-le(alpha, gamma))
  • ord-segment-is-set — forall([alpha in ord], ord-segment(alpha) in set)
  • ord-segment-limit — forall([lambda], limit-ord(lambda) implies forall([x], ord-lt(x, lambda) implies forsome([beta], ord-lt(x, beta) and ord-lt(beta, lambda))))
  • ord-segment-membership — forall([alpha, x], alpha in ord implies x in ord-segment(alpha) iff ord-lt(x, alpha))
  • ord-segment-succ — forall([alpha in ord, x], x in ord-segment(succ_ord(alpha)) iff x in ord-segment(alpha) or x = alpha)
  • ord-segment-zero — ord-segment(0) = empty-set
  • ord-succ-above — forall([alpha in ord], ord-lt(alpha, succ_ord(alpha)))
  • ord-succ-immediate — forall([alpha, beta], alpha in ord and beta in ord and ord-lt(alpha, beta) implies ord-le(succ_ord(alpha), beta))
  • ord-succ-in — forall([alpha in ord], succ_ord(alpha) in ord)
  • ord-succ-injective — forall([alpha, beta], alpha in ord and beta in ord and succ_ord(alpha) = succ_ord(beta) implies alpha = beta)
  • ord-succ-nn — forall([n in nn], succ_ord(n) = succ(n))
  • ord-zero-least — forall([alpha in ord], ord-le(0, alpha))
  • pairing — forall([a, b], a in set and b in set implies pair(a, b) in set)
  • pairing-membership — forall([a, b], a in set and b in set implies forall([x], x in pair(a, b) iff x = a or x = b))
  • partial-fun-binary-membership — forall([a, c, f], f in partial-fun(a, c) iff forsome([b in power(a)], f in fun(b, c)))
  • partial-fun-binary-sethood — forall([a, c], a in set and c in set implies partial-fun(a, c) in set)
  • partial-fun-membership — forall([a, f], f in partial-fun(a) iff forsome([b in power(a)], f in fun(b)))
  • power-neg — forall([x, n], x in cc and n in nn and not(x = 0) implies x ^ (-n) = recip(x ^ n))
  • power-set — forall([a in set], power(a) in set)
  • power-set-membership — forall([a, x], x in power(a) iff x in set and forall([z in x], z in a))
  • power-succ — forall([x, n], x in cc and n in nn implies x ^ succ(n) = x * x ^ n)
  • power-typing-nonneg — forall([x, n], x in cc and n in nn implies x ^ n in cc)
  • power-zero — forall([x in cc], x ^ 0 = 1)
  • qq-add-assoc — forall([a, b, c], a in qq and b in qq and c in qq implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
  • qq-add-closed — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a + b in qq)
  • qq-add-comm — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a + b = b + a)
  • qq-add-zero — forall([a in qq], a + 0 = a)

  • qq-is-set — qq in set
  • qq-mul-assoc — forall([a, b, c], a in qq and b in qq and c in qq implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
  • qq-mul-closed — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a * b in qq)
  • qq-mul-comm — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a * b = b * a)
  • qq-neg-closed — forall([a in qq], -a in qq)
  • qq-neg-inverse — forall([a in qq], a + -a = 0)

  • qq-one-mul — forall([a in qq], 1 * a = a)
  • qq-recip-closed — forall([a], a in qq and not(a = 0) implies recip(a) in qq)
  • qq-recip-inverse — forall([a], a in qq and not(a = 0) implies a * recip(a) = 1)
  • qq-subset-rr — forall([n in qq], n in rr)
  • qq-zero-in — 0 in qq
  • quasi-eq-def — forall([a, b], a = a implies a == b iff a = b)
  • quasi-eq-reflexivity — forall([a], a == a)
  • quasi-eq-subst-membership — forall([a, b, s], a == b and b in s implies a in s) [warrant: well-known]
  • quasi-eq-symmetry — forall([a, b], a == b implies b == a)
  • quasi-eq-transitivity — forall([a, b, c], a == b and b == c implies a == c)
  • res-apply — forall([a, b, f, x], f in fun(a) and b subset a and x in b implies (res(f, b))(x) = f(x))
  • res-codomain — forall([a, b, c, f], f in fun(a, c) and b subset a implies res(f, b) in fun(b, c))
  • res-typing — forall([a, b, f], f in fun(a) and b subset a implies res(f, b) in fun(b))

  • rr-add-closed — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a + b in rr)

Emitted by def-/declare- forms (IS-X folding, accessor laws, view typing). Name new vocabulary; add no strength.

  • < — forall([x, y], x < y iff x <= y and not(x = y))

  • is-abelian-group — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) iff length(s) = 4 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and inv(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(opr(s), iden(s), inv(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(opr(s), carr(s)))
  • is-absolutely-summable — forall([grp, f], is-absolutely-summable(grp, f) iff esum(vnb-lambda(i, dom(f), (nrm(grp))(f(i)))) in rr)
  • is-algebra-of-sets — forall([omega, ca], is-algebra-of-sets(omega, ca) iff omega in set and ca subset power(omega) and forsome([a_], a_ in ca) and forall([a_ in ca], complement-in(omega, a_) in ca) and forall([a_ in ca, b_ in ca], union(a_, b_) in ca) and forall([a_ in ca, b_ in ca], intersection(a_, b_) in ca))
  • is-antiderivable — forall([phi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) iff forsome([f_], is-antiderivative(f_, phi, a, b)))
  • is-antiderivative — forall([f, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and phi in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b and forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) and forall([th_], th_ in rr and a < th_ and th_ < b implies is-diff-at(f, th_, phi(th_))))
  • is-associative — forall([op, crr], is-associative(op, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], op(op(u, v), w) = op(u, op(v, w))))
  • is-bounded-linear-functional — forall([m, f], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f) iff is-linear-functional(m, f) and forsome([c_ in rr], 0 <= c_ and forall([x_ in vec(m)], abs(f(x_)) <= c_ * (vnrm(m))(x_))))
  • is-bounded-linear-functional-on — forall([m, s, f], is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) iff is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) and forsome([c_ in rr], 0 <= c_ and forall([x_ in s], abs(f(x_)) <= c_ * (vnrm(m))(x_))))
  • is-bounded-metric-space — forall([s], is-bounded-metric-space(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forsome([b in rr], forall([x, y], x in pts(s) implies y in pts(s) implies (dist(s))(x, y) <= b)))
  • is-c-metric-space — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and dists(s) in fun(nn, fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), rr)) and forall([k_ in nn], is-pseudometric((dists(s))(k_), pts(s))) and forall([u in pts(s), v in pts(s)], forall([k_ in nn], ((dists(s))(k_))(u, v) = 0) implies u = v))
  • is-caratheodory-measurable — forall([omega, mu, es], is-caratheodory-measurable(omega, mu, es) iff es subset omega and forall([a_ in power(omega)], mu(a_) = eplus(mu(intersection(a_, es)), mu(difference(a_, es)))))
  • is-cauchy-seq — forall([s, f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], n <= m and n <= n_ implies (dist(s))(f(m), f(n_)) <= eps))))
  • is-chain — forall([grd, porel, ch], is-chain(grd, porel, ch) iff ch subset grd and forall([x in ch, y in ch], [x, y] in porel or [y, x] in porel))
  • is-closed — forall([s, a], is-closed(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and is-open(s, complement-in(pts(s), a)))
  • is-comm-monoid — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) iff length(s) = 3 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(opr(s), carr(s)))
  • is-commutative — forall([op, crr], is-commutative(op, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr], op(u, v) = op(v, u)))
  • is-commutative-ring-def — forall([s], is-commutative-ring(s) iff is-ring(s) and forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, b) = (mul(s))(b, a)))
  • is-compact — forall([s], is-compact(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([c], is-open-cover(s, c) implies forsome([f], f subset c and card(f) in nn and is-open-cover(s, f))))
  • is-complete — forall([s], is-complete(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) implies converges(s, f)))
  • is-complex-inner-product-space — forall([s], is-complex-inner-product-space(s) iff length(s) = 7 and is-ring(scal(s)) and vec(s) in set and vadd(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vzero(s) in vec(s) and vneg(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and act(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(scal(s)), vec(s)), vec(s)) and ip(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), cc) and is-associative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-commutative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-identity(vadd(s), vzero(s), vec(s)) and has-inverses(vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), vec(s)) and scal(s) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(cc-normed-field) and carr(scal(s)) = cc and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(r_, (vadd(s))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(r_, y_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((add(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((mul(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(s))(r_, (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_) and forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s), z_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))((vadd(s))(x_, y_), z_) = (ip(s))(x_, z_) + (ip(s))(y_, z_)) and forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))((act(s))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * (ip(s))(x_, y_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))(y_, x_) = conjugate((ip(s))(x_, y_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))(x_, x_) in rr) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], 0 <= (ip(s))(x_, x_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (ip(s))(x_, x_) = 0 iff x_ = vzero(s)))
  • is-cont-lin — forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], is-cont-lin(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) iff is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) and forall([seq in fun(nn, vec(m1)), lim in vec(m1)], fr-conv(m1, fam1, seq, lim) implies fr-conv(m2, fam2, vnb-lambda(i, nn, tt(seq(i))), tt(lim))))
  • is-continuous — forall([s, t, f], is-continuous(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([a in pts(s)], is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a)))
  • is-continuous-at — forall([s, t, f, a], is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and a in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([delta], pos-rr(delta) and forall([b in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b) <= delta implies (dist(t))(f(a), f(b)) <= eps))))
  • is-countable-pseudometric-family — forall([fam, ground], is-countable-pseudometric-family(fam, ground) iff forall([n_ in nn], is-pseudometric-space(fam(n_)) and pts(fam(n_)) == ground))
  • is-dense-seq — forall([s, dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) iff is-metric-space(s) and dseq in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([m in nn], (dist(s))(x, dseq(m)) < eps)))
  • is-diagonal — forall([a, m, n, d], is-diagonal(a, m, n, d) iff forall([i, j], i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(i = j) implies entry(d, i, j) = zero(a)))
  • is-diff-at — forall([f, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and l in rr and forsome([phi in fun(rr, rr)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, phi, a) and phi(a) = l and forall([x in rr], f(x) - f(a) = phi(x) * (x - a))))
  • is-diff-at-v — forall([m, f, a, l], is-diff-at-v(m, f, a, l) iff is-normed-vector-space(m) and f in fun(rr, vec(m)) and a in rr and l in vec(m) and forsome([phi in fun(rr, vec(m))], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, nvs-metric-space(m), phi, a) and phi(a) = l and forall([x_ in rr], (vadd(m))(f(x_), (vneg(m))(f(a))) = (act(m))(x_ - a, phi(x_)))))
  • is-dir-diff-at — forall([m, f, a, eta, dl], is-dir-diff-at(m, f, a, eta, dl) iff is-diff-at(seg-curve(m, f, a, eta), 0, dl))
  • is-distributive — forall([addop, mulop, crr], is-distributive(addop, mulop, crr) iff forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], mulop(u, addop(v, w)) = addop(mulop(u, v), mulop(u, w)) and mulop(addop(u, v), w) = addop(mulop(u, w), mulop(v, w))))
  • is-eps-cauchy-seq — forall([s, eps, y], is-eps-cauchy-seq(s, eps, y) iff is-metric-space(s) and y in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], (dist(s))(y(m), y(n_)) <= eps))
  • is-equicontinuous — forall([s, t, fam], is-equicontinuous(s, t, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), pts(t))) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([del], pos-rr(del) and forall([k in nn, y in pts(s)], (dist(s))(x, y) < del implies (dist(t))((fam(k))(x), (fam(k))(y)) < eps))))
  • is-equivalence — forall([rho, crr], is-equivalence(rho, crr) iff rho in power(cartesian(crr, crr)) and forall([u in crr], [u, u] in rho) and forall([u in crr, v in crr], [u, v] in rho implies [v, u] in rho) and forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], [u, v] in rho and [v, w] in rho implies [u, w] in rho))
  • is-euclidean-ring-def — forall([s], is-euclidean-ring(s) iff is-integral-domain(s) and forsome([dg in fun(carr(s), nn)], forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], not(b = zero(s)) implies forsome([q in carr(s), r in carr(s)], a = (add(s))((mul(s))(q, b), r) and (r = zero(s) or succ(dg(r)) <= dg(b))))))
  • is-field — forall([s], is-field(s) iff length(s) = 8 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and non-zero(s) in set and non-zero(s) = difference(carr(s), singleton(zero(s))) and recip(s) in fun(non-zero(s), non-zero(s)) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)))
  • is-field-ring-def — forall([s], is-field-ring(s) iff is-commutative-ring(s) and not(one(s) = zero(s)) and forall([a_ in carr(s)], not(a_ = zero(s)) implies forsome([b_ in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a_, b_) = one(s))))
  • is-finite-cover — forall([c, a], is-finite-cover(c, a) iff c in set and card(c) in nn and a subset big-union(u, c, u))
  • is-finite-dimensional — forall([m], is-finite-dimensional(m) iff is-vector-space(m) and is-noetherian(m))
  • is-frechet-structure — forall([m, fam], is-frechet-structure(m, fam) iff is-seminorm-family(m, fam) and forall([seq in fun(nn, vec(m))], fr-cauchy(m, fam, seq) implies forsome([lim in vec(m)], fr-conv(m, fam, seq, lim))))
  • is-gauge-countable — forall([s], is-gauge-countable(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forsome([fam], is-countable-pseudometric-family(fam, pts(s)) and pseudo-gauge-top(fam, pts(s)) == s))
  • is-group — forall([s], is-group(s) iff length(s) = 4 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and inv(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(opr(s), iden(s), inv(s), carr(s)))
  • is-group-norm — forall([nm, op, invop, unit, crr], is-group-norm(nm, op, invop, unit, crr) iff nm in fun(crr, rr) and forall([u in crr], 0 <= nm(u) and (nm(u) = 0 iff u = unit) and nm(invop(u)) = nm(u) and forall([v in crr], nm(op(u, v)) <= nm(u) + nm(v))))
  • is-hausdorff — forall([s], is-hausdorff(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forall([a, b], a in pts(s) implies b in pts(s) implies not(a == b) implies forsome([u, v], u in opens(s) and v in opens(s) and a in u and b in v and intersection(u, v) == empty-set)))
  • is-hom-abelian-group-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-abelian-group(a, b, f) iff is-abelian-group(a) and is-abelian-group(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((inv(a))(x1_)) = (inv(b))(f(x1_))))

  • is-hom-complex-inner-product-space-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-complex-inner-product-space(a, b, f) iff is-complex-inner-product-space(a) and is-complex-inner-product-space(b) and f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and scal(a) = scal(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], f((vadd(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (vadd(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(vzero(a)) = vzero(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], f((vneg(a))(x1_)) = (vneg(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(scal(a)), x2_ in vec(a)], f((act(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (act(b))(x1_, f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], (ip(a))(x1_, x2_) = (ip(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))

  • is-hom-field-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-field(a, b, f) iff is-field(a) and is-field(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((add(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (add(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((mul(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (mul(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((neg(a))(x1_)) = (neg(b))(f(x1_))) and f(zero(a)) = zero(b) and f(one(a)) = one(b) and forall([x1_ in non-zero(a)], f((recip(a))(x1_)) = (recip(b))(f(x1_))))

  • is-hom-group-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-group(a, b, f) iff is-group(a) and is-group(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(iden(a)) = iden(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((inv(a))(x1_)) = (inv(b))(f(x1_))))

  • is-hom-measurable-space-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-measurable-space(a, b, f) iff is-measurable-space(a) and is-measurable-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and sigma(a) = sigma(b))
