  • is-hom-normed-field-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-field(a, b, f) iff is-normed-field(a) and is-normed-field(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((add(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (add(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((mul(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (mul(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((neg(a))(x1_)) = (neg(b))(f(x1_))) and f(zero(a)) = zero(b) and f(one(a)) = one(b) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], (fnrm(a))(x1_) = (fnrm(b))(f(x1_))))
  • is-hom-normed-vector-space-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-vector-space(a, b, f) iff is-normed-vector-space(a) and is-normed-vector-space(b) and f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and scal(a) = scal(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a), x2_ in vec(a)], f((vadd(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (vadd(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and f(vzero(a)) = vzero(b) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], f((vneg(a))(x1_)) = (vneg(b))(f(x1_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(scal(a)), x2_ in vec(a)], f((act(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (act(b))(x1_, f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in vec(a)], (vnrm(a))(x1_) = (vnrm(b))(f(x1_))))
  • is-hom-pid-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-pid(a, b, f) iff is-pid(a) and is-pid(b) and is-hom-integral-domain(a, b, f))
  • is-hom-pseudometric-space-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-pseudometric-space(a, b, f) iff is-pseudometric-space(a) and is-pseudometric-space(b) and f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and forall([x1_ in pts(a), x2_ in pts(a)], (dist(a))(x1_, x2_) = (dist(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))
  • is-hom-ring-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-ring(a, b, f) iff is-ring(a) and is-ring(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((add(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (add(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((mul(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (mul(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))) and forall([x1_ in carr(a)], f((neg(a))(x1_)) = (neg(b))(f(x1_))) and f(zero(a)) = zero(b) and f(one(a)) = one(b))
  • is-hom-ringoid-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-ringoid(a, b, f) iff is-ringoid(a) and is-ringoid(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and is-hom-ring(ringoid-as-ring(a), ringoid-as-ring(b), f) and forall([x_ in idl(a)], f(x_) in idl(b)))
  • is-hom-semigroup-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-semigroup(a, b, f) iff is-semigroup(a) and is-semigroup(b) and f in fun(carr(a), carr(b)) and forall([x1_ in carr(a), x2_ in carr(a)], f((opr(a))(x1_, x2_)) = (opr(b))(f(x1_), f(x2_))))
  • is-hom-setoid-def — forall([a, b, f1, f2], is-hom-setoid(a, b, f1, f2) iff is-setoid(a) and is-setoid(b) and f1 in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and f2 in fun(rel(a), rel(b)))
  • is-hom-top-space-def — forall([s, t, f], is-hom-top-space(s, t, f) iff is-top-space(s) and is-top-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([u in opens(t)], preimage(s, f, u) in opens(s)))
  • is-hom-vector-space-def — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-vector-space(a, b, f) iff is-vector-space(a) and is-vector-space(b) and is-hom-module(a, b, f))
  • is-ideal — forall([s, i], is-ideal(s, i) iff is-commutative-ring(s) and i subset carr(s) and zero(s) in i and forall([a in i, b in i], (add(s))(a, b) in i) and forall([a in i], (neg(s))(a) in i) and forall([r in carr(s), a in i], (mul(s))(r, a) in i))
  • is-ideal-in — forall([i_, carr_, add_, mul_, neg_, zero_], is-ideal-in(i_, carr_, add_, mul_, neg_, zero_) iff i_ subset carr_ and zero_ in i_ and forall([a, b], a in i_ implies b in i_ implies add_(a, b) in i_) and forall([a in i_], neg_(a) in i_) and forall([x_, a], x_ in carr_ implies a in i_ implies mul_(x_, a) in i_) and forall([x_, a], x_ in carr_ implies a in i_ implies mul_(a, x_) in i_))
  • is-identity — forall([op, unit, crr], is-identity(op, unit, crr) iff forall([u in crr], op(unit, u) = u and op(u, unit) = u))
  • is-integral-domain-def — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) iff is-commutative-ring(s) and not(one(s) = zero(s)) and forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, b) = zero(s) implies a = zero(s) or b = zero(s)))
  • is-invertible-mat — forall([a, n, u], is-invertible-mat(a, n, u) iff u in mat(n, n, carr(a)) and forsome([v in mat(n, n, carr(a))], matmul(a, u, v) = identmat(a, n) and matmul(a, v, u) = identmat(a, n)))
  • is-isometry — forall([s, t, f], is-isometry(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(t))(f(x), f(y)) = (dist(s))(x, y)))
  • is-k-linear — forall([m, f], is-k-linear(m, f) iff is-module(m) and f in fun(vec(m), carr(scal(m))) and forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], f((vadd(m))(x, y)) = (add(scal(m)))(f(x), f(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in vec(m)], f((act(m))(lam, x)) = (mul(scal(m)))(lam, f(x))))
  • is-k-linear-on — forall([m, s, f], is-k-linear-on(m, s, f) iff is-module(m) and is-submodule(m, s) and f in fun(s, carr(scal(m))) and forall([x in s, y in s], f((vadd(m))(x, y)) = (add(scal(m)))(f(x), f(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in s], f((act(m))(lam, x)) = (mul(scal(m)))(lam, f(x))))
  • is-linear-functional — forall([m, f], is-linear-functional(m, f) iff f in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], f((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = f(x_) + f(y_)) and forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], f((act(m))(r_, x_)) = r_ * f(x_)))
  • is-linear-functional-on — forall([m, s, f], is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) iff f in fun(s, rr) and forall([x_ in s, y_ in s], f((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = f(x_) + f(y_)) and forall([r_ in rr, x_ in s], f((act(m))(r_, x_)) = r_ * f(x_)))
  • is-linear-map — forall([m1, m2, tt], is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) iff is-module(m1) and is-module(m2) and scal(m1) = scal(m2) and tt in fun(vec(m1), vec(m2)) and forall([x in vec(m1), y in vec(m1)], tt((vadd(m1))(x, y)) = (vadd(m2))(tt(x), tt(y))) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m1)), x in vec(m1)], tt((act(m1))(lam, x)) = (act(m2))(lam, tt(x))))
  • is-maximal — forall([grd, porel, mx], is-maximal(grd, porel, mx) iff mx in grd and forall([y in grd], [mx, y] in porel implies [y, mx] in porel))
  • is-meager — forall([s, a], is-meager(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and forsome([ee in fun(nn, power(pts(s)))], forall([n in nn], is-nowhere-dense(s, ee(n))) and a subset big-union(n, nn, ee(n))))
  • is-measurable-fn — forall([omega, ca, f], is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and f in fun(omega, rr-pos-star) and forall([alpha_ in rr], {x_ in omega: alpha_ < f(x_)} in ca))
  • is-measurable-map — forall([omega, ca, omega2, cb, f], is-measurable-map(omega, ca, omega2, cb, f) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and is-sigma-algebra(omega2, cb) and f in fun(omega, omega2) and forall([b_ in cb], {x_ in omega: f(x_) in b_} in ca))
  • is-measurable-space — forall([s], is-measurable-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and sigma(s) in power(power(pts(s))) and is-sigma-algebra(pts(s), sigma(s)))
  • is-measure — forall([omega, ca, mu], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) iff is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) and mu in fun(ca, rr-pos-star) and mu(empty-set) = 0 and forall([f_ in fun(nn, ca)], forall([i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies not(i_ = j_) implies intersection(f_(i_), f_(j_)) = empty-set) implies mu(big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_))) = esum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, mu(f_(n_))))))
  • is-measure-space — forall([s], is-measure-space(s) iff length(s) = 3 and pts(s) in set and sigma(s) in power(power(pts(s))) and meas(s) in fun(sigma(s), rr-pos-star) and is-measure(pts(s), sigma(s), meas(s)))
  • is-metric — forall([dst, crr], is-metric(dst, crr) iff forall([u in crr], dst(u, u) = 0 and forall([v in crr], 0 <= dst(u, v) and (dst(u, v) = 0 implies u = v) and dst(u, v) = dst(v, u) and forall([w in crr], dst(u, w) <= dst(u, v) + dst(v, w)))))
  • is-metric-space — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and dist(s) in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), rr) and is-metric(dist(s), pts(s)))
  • is-metrizable-top-space-def — forall([s], is-metrizable-top-space(s) iff is-top-space(s) and forsome([md], is-metric-space(md) and s == [pts(md), {u in power(pts(md)): is-open(md, u)}]))
  • is-module — forall([s], is-module(s) iff length(s) = 6 and is-ring(scal(s)) and vec(s) in set and vadd(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vzero(s) in vec(s) and vneg(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and act(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(scal(s)), vec(s)), vec(s)) and is-associative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-commutative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-identity(vadd(s), vzero(s), vec(s)) and has-inverses(vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), vec(s)) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(r_, (vadd(s))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(r_, y_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((add(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((mul(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(s))(r_, (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_))
  • is-monoid — forall([s], is-monoid(s) iff length(s) = 3 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)))
  • is-ms-sequence — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) iff forall([n in nn], is-metric-space(ms(n))))
  • is-noetherian — forall([m], is-noetherian(m) iff is-module(m) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, power(vec(m)))], forall([n_ in nn], is-submodule(m, f_(n_))) and forall([n_ in nn], f_(n_) subset f_(succ(n_))) implies forsome([k_ in nn], forall([n_], n_ in nn and k_ <= n_ implies f_(n_) = f_(k_)))))
  • is-nonmeager — forall([s, a], is-nonmeager(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and not(is-meager(s, a)))
  • is-norm — forall([nm, addop, mulop, zr, crr], is-norm(nm, addop, mulop, zr, crr) iff nm in fun(crr, rr) and forall([a in crr], 0 <= nm(a) and (nm(a) = 0 iff a = zr) and forall([b in crr], nm(mulop(a, b)) = nm(a) * nm(b) and nm(addop(a, b)) <= nm(a) + nm(b))))
  • is-normed-ag — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) iff length(s) = 5 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and iden(s) in carr(s) and inv(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and nrm(s) in fun(carr(s), rr) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(opr(s), iden(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(opr(s), iden(s), inv(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(opr(s), carr(s)) and is-group-norm(nrm(s), opr(s), inv(s), iden(s), carr(s)))
  • is-normed-field — forall([s], is-normed-field(s) iff length(s) = 7 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and fnrm(s) in fun(carr(s), rr) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)) and is-norm(fnrm(s), add(s), mul(s), zero(s), carr(s)))
  • is-normed-vector-space — forall([s], is-normed-vector-space(s) iff length(s) = 7 and is-ring(scal(s)) and vec(s) in set and vadd(s) in fun(cartesian(vec(s), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vzero(s) in vec(s) and vneg(s) in fun(vec(s), vec(s)) and act(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(scal(s)), vec(s)), vec(s)) and vnrm(s) in fun(vec(s), rr) and is-associative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-commutative(vadd(s), vec(s)) and is-identity(vadd(s), vzero(s), vec(s)) and has-inverses(vadd(s), vzero(s), vneg(s), vec(s)) and scal(s) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(r_, (vadd(s))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(r_, y_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((add(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(s))((act(s))(r_, x_), (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), s_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))((mul(scal(s)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(s))(r_, (act(s))(s_, x_))) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (act(s))(one(scal(s)), x_) = x_) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], 0 <= (vnrm(s))(x_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s)], (vnrm(s))(x_) = 0 iff x_ = vzero(s)) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(s)), x_ in vec(s)], (vnrm(s))((act(s))(r_, x_)) = abs(r_) * (vnrm(s))(x_)) and forall([x_ in vec(s), y_ in vec(s)], (vnrm(s))((vadd(s))(x_, y_)) <= (vnrm(s))(x_) + (vnrm(s))(y_)))
  • is-nowhere-dense — forall([s, a], is-nowhere-dense(s, a) iff is-metric-space(s) and a subset pts(s) and interior(s, closure(s, a)) = empty-set)
  • is-open — forall([s, u], is-open(s, u) iff is-metric-space(s) and u subset pts(s) and forall([y in u], forsome([r], pos-rr(r) and ball(s, y, r) subset u)))
  • is-open-cover — forall([s, c], is-open-cover(s, c) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([u in c], is-open(s, u)) and big-union(u, c, u) == pts(s))
  • is-open-gauge — forall([fam, ground, u], is-open-gauge(fam, ground, u) iff u subset ground and forall([y in u], forsome([k_ in nn, r], pos-rr(r) and forall([x in ground], forall([n_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies (dist(fam(n_)))(y, x) < r) implies x in u))))
  • is-open-lin-map — forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], is-open-lin-map(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) iff is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) and forall([n in nn, eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn, eps_], pos-rr(eps_) and fr-ball(m2, fam2, n_, eps_) subset image(tt, fr-ball(m1, fam1, n, eps)))))
  • is-ord — forall([x], is-ord(x) iff x in ord)
  • is-outer-measure — forall([omega, mu], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) iff omega in set and mu in fun(power(omega), rr-pos-star) and mu(empty-set) = 0 and forall([a_, b_], a_ in power(omega) implies b_ in power(omega) implies a_ subset b_ implies mu(a_) <= mu(b_)) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, power(omega))], mu(big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_))) <= esum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, mu(f_(n_))))))
  • is-partial-order — forall([grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) iff grd in set and porel in set and forall([x in grd], [x, x] in porel) and forall([x in grd, y in grd], [x, y] in porel and [y, x] in porel implies x = y) and forall([x in grd, y in grd, z in grd], [x, y] in porel and [y, z] in porel implies [x, z] in porel))
  • is-pid-def — forall([s], is-pid(s) iff is-integral-domain(s) and forall([i], is-ideal(s, i) implies forsome([a in carr(s)], i = principal-ideal(s, a))))
  • is-pseudometric — forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) iff forall([u in crr], dst(u, u) = 0 and forall([v in crr], 0 <= dst(u, v) and dst(u, v) = dst(v, u) and forall([w in crr], dst(u, w) <= dst(u, v) + dst(v, w)))))
  • is-pseudometric-space — forall([s], is-pseudometric-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and dist(s) in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), rr) and is-pseudometric(dist(s), pts(s)))
  • is-ptwise-cauchy — forall([s, fam], is-ptwise-cauchy(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([l], l in nn and cap <= l implies abs((fam(k))(x) - (fam(l))(x)) < eps)))))
  • is-r-net — forall([s, f, a, r], is-r-net(s, f, a, r) iff forall([p in a], forsome([c in f], (dist(s))(c, p) <= r and not((dist(s))(c, p) = r))))
  • is-ring — forall([s], is-ring(s) iff length(s) = 6 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)))
  • is-ringoid — forall([s], is-ringoid(s) iff length(s) = 7 and carr(s) in set and add(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and mul(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and neg(s) in fun(carr(s), carr(s)) and zero(s) in carr(s) and one(s) in carr(s) and idl(s) in power(carr(s)) and is-associative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-commutative(add(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(add(s), zero(s), carr(s)) and has-inverses(add(s), zero(s), neg(s), carr(s)) and is-associative(mul(s), carr(s)) and is-identity(mul(s), one(s), carr(s)) and is-distributive(add(s), mul(s), carr(s)) and is-ideal-in(idl(s), carr(s), add(s), mul(s), neg(s), zero(s)))
  • is-semigroup — forall([s], is-semigroup(s) iff length(s) = 2 and carr(s) in set and opr(s) in fun(cartesian(carr(s), carr(s)), carr(s)) and is-associative(opr(s), carr(s)))
  • is-seminorm — forall([m, p], is-seminorm(m, p) iff is-module(m) and p in fun(vec(m), rr) and forall([x in vec(m)], 0 <= p(x)) and forall([x in vec(m), y in vec(m)], p((vadd(m))(x, y)) <= p(x) + p(y)) and forsome([fld], is-normed-field(fld) and scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(fld) and forall([lam in carr(scal(m)), x in vec(m)], p((act(m))(lam, x)) = (fnrm(fld))(lam) * p(x))))
  • is-seminorm-family — forall([m, fam], is-seminorm-family(m, fam) iff is-module(m) and forsome([fld], is-normed-field(fld) and scal(m) = normed-field-as-commutative-ring(fld)) and fam in fun(nn, fun(vec(m), rr)) and forall([k in nn], is-seminorm(m, fam(k))) and forall([x in vec(m)], not(x = vzero(m)) implies forsome([k in nn], not((fam(k))(x) = 0))))
  • is-separable — forall([s], is-separable(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forsome([dseq in fun(nn, pts(s))], forall([x in pts(s), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], (dist(s))(x, dseq(n)) < eps))))
  • is-set — forall([x], is-set(x) iff x in set)
  • is-setoid — forall([s], is-setoid(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and rel(s) in set and is-equivalence(rel(s), pts(s)))
  • is-sigma-algebra — forall([omega, ca], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) iff omega in set and ca subset power(omega) and omega in ca and forall([a_ in ca], complement-in(omega, a_) in ca) and forall([f_ in fun(nn, ca)], big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_)) in ca))
  • is-simple-fn — forall([omega, ca, f], is-simple-fn(omega, ca, f) iff is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) and card(image(f, omega)) in nn and forall([x_ in omega], f(x_) in rr))
  • is-strictly-below — forall([porel, x, y], is-strictly-below(porel, x, y) iff [x, y] in porel and not([y, x] in porel))
  • is-strictly-increasing-on — forall([f, s], is-strictly-increasing-on(f, s) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and s subset rr and forall([u in s, v in s], u < v implies f(u) < f(v)))
  • is-submodule — forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) iff s subset vec(m) and vzero(m) in s and forall([x_ in s, y_ in s], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in s) and forall([x_ in s], (vneg(m))(x_) in s) and forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in s], (act(m))(r_, x_) in s))
  • is-subsequence — forall([s, y, f], is-subsequence(s, y, f) iff f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and y = subseq(f, phi)))
  • is-subspace — forall([m, s], is-subspace(m, s) iff is-submodule(m, s))
  • is-summable — forall([grp, f], is-summable(grp, f) iff forsome([r in carr(grp)], sums-to(grp, f, r)))
  • is-top-space — forall([s], is-top-space(s) iff length(s) = 2 and pts(s) in set and opens(s) in power(power(pts(s))) and empty-set in opens(s) and pts(s) in opens(s) and forall([u in opens(s), v in opens(s)], intersection(u, v) in opens(s)) and forall([fam], fam subset opens(s) implies big-union(u, fam, u) in opens(s)))
  • is-unif-cauchy — forall([s, fam], is-unif-cauchy(s, fam) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([l], l in nn and cap <= l implies forall([x in pts(s)], abs((fam(k))(x) - (fam(l))(x)) < eps))))))
  • is-uniformly-continuous — forall([s, t, f], is-uniformly-continuous(s, t, f) iff is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([delta], pos-rr(delta) and forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b) <= delta implies (dist(t))(f(a), f(b)) <= eps))))
  • is-upper-bound — forall([grd, porel, ch, b], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch, b) iff b in grd and forall([x in ch], [x, b] in porel))
  • is-vector-space-def — forall([s], is-vector-space(s) iff is-module(s) and is-field-ring(scal(s)))
  • keep-set-membership — forall([phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha, y_], y_ in keep-set(phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha) iff y_ in grd and y_ = phi(alpha) and forall([z in kset], [z, y_] in porel))
  • lastcoeff-set-membership — forall([md, p, u, sm, r_], r_ in lastcoeff-set(md, p, u, sm) iff r_ in carr(scal(md)) and forsome([c_ in mat(1, succ(p), carr(scal(md)))], entry(c_, 1, succ(p)) = r_ and entry(matact(md, c_, u), 1, 1) in sm))
  • length-cons — forall([x, l], length(cons(x, l)) = succ(length(l)))
  • limit-ord-iff — forall([lambda], limit-ord(lambda) iff lambda in ord and not(lambda = 0) and not(forsome([alpha in ord], lambda = succ_ord(alpha))))
  • little-o-at — forall([g, a], little-o-at(g, a) iff g in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and forsome([eps in fun(rr, rr)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, eps, a) and eps(a) = 0 and forall([x in rr], g(x) = eps(x) * (x - a))))
  • magnitude-def — forall([z], magnitude(z) == sqrt(real-part(z) * real-part(z) + imag-part(z) * imag-part(z)))
  • makeset-cons — forall([x, l], make-set(cons(x, l)) = union(pair(x, x), make-set(l)))
  • mat-equiv — forall([a, m, n, c, d], mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, d) iff forsome([u], is-invertible-mat(a, m, u) and forsome([v], is-invertible-mat(a, n, v) and d = matmul(a, matmul(a, u, c), v))))
  • matrix-membership — forall([s, m], m in matrix(s) iff m in tuples(tuples(s)) and forall([i], i in nn and 1 <= i and i <= length(m) implies forall([j], j in nn and 1 <= j and j <= length(m) implies length(nth(i, m)) = length(nth(j, m)))))
  • measurable-space-class — forall([s], s in measurable-space iff is-measurable-space(s))

  • smith-staircase — forall([a, m, n, d, k], smith-staircase(a, m, n, d, k) iff is-diagonal(a, m, n, d) and k <= m and k <= n and forall([i_ in interval(1, k)], not(entry(d, i_, i_) = zero(a))) and forall([i_ in interval(1, m)], not(i_ <= k) implies forall([j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(d, i_, j_) = zero(a))))
  • span-membership — forall([md, n, u, x_], x_ in span(md, n, u) iff x_ in vec(md) and forsome([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], x_ = entry(matact(md, c_, u), 1, 1)))
  • spans — forall([md, n, u, sm], spans(md, n, u, sm) iff forall([j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(u, j_, 1) in sm) and forall([x_ in sm], forsome([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], x_ = entry(matact(md, c_, u), 1, 1))))
  • sqn-membership — forall([a, f_], f_ in sqn(a) iff f_ in fun(nn, a))
  • sqn-sethood — forall([a in set], sqn(a) in set)
  • strictly-mono-nn — forall([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) iff phi in fun(nn, nn) and forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], m < n_ implies phi(m) < phi(n_)))
  • submodule-act-closed — forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in s], (act(m))(r_, x_) in s))
  • submodule-vadd-closed — forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) implies forall([x_ in s, y_ in s], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in s))
  • submodule-vneg-closed — forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) implies forall([x_ in s], (vneg(m))(x_) in s))
  • submodule-vzero-in — forall([m, s], is-submodule(m, s) implies vzero(m) in s)
  • subsqn — forall([y, f], subsqn(y, f) iff forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and y = subseq(f, phi)))
  • sum-ag-as-reduce-ring-additive-ag — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(r)), n], n in nn and 1 <= n implies sum-ag(ring-additive-ag(r), f, n) = reduce(add(r), f, n)))
  • sum-ag-singleton-ring-additive-ag — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(r))], sum-ag(ring-additive-ag(r), f, 1) = f(0)))
  • sum-ag-succ — forall([ag, f, n in nn], sum-ag(ag, f, succ(n)) == (opr(ag))(sum-ag(ag, f, n), f(n)))
  • sum-ag-type-ring-additive-ag — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(r)), n in nn], sum-ag(ring-additive-ag(r), f, n) in carr(r)))
  • sum-ag-zero — forall([ag, f], sum-ag(ag, f, 0) == iden(ag))
  • sum-succ — forall([r, f, n in nn], sum(r, f, succ(n)) == (add(r))(sum(r, f, n), f(n)))
  • sum-zero — forall([r, f], sum(r, f, 0) == zero(r))
  • summable-weight — forall([w], summable-weight(w) iff w in fun(nn, rr) and forall([n in nn], 0 < w(n)) and series-converges(w))
  • sums-to — forall([grp, f, r], sums-to(grp, f, r) iff forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([fin in set], card(fin) in nn and fin subset dom(f) and forall([ext], ext in set and card(ext) in nn and fin subset ext and ext subset dom(f) implies (nrm(grp))((opr(grp))(r, (inv(grp))(finsum(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(grp), f, ext)))) < eps))))
  • taylor-differentiable — forall([f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) iff forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t in ccint(a, x)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, nth-deriv(f, k), t))) and forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t], a < t and t < x implies is-diff-at(nth-deriv(f, k), t, (nth-deriv(f, succ(k)))(t)))))
  • taylor-differentiable-v — forall([m, f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable-v(m, f, a, x, n) iff forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t in ccint(a, x)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, nvs-metric-space(m), nth-deriv-v(m, f, k), t))) and forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies forall([t], a < t and t < x implies is-diff-at-v(m, nth-deriv-v(m, f, k), t, (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(k)))(t)))))
  • taylor-poly-v-succ — forall([m, f, a, x, n in nn], taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, succ(n)) == (vadd(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n), (act(m))((x - a) ^ succ(n) * recip(factorial(succ(n))), (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(a))))
  • taylor-poly-v-zero — forall([m, f, a, x], taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, 0) == (act(m))((x - a) ^ 0 * recip(factorial(0)), (nth-deriv-v(m, f, 0))(a)))
  • top-space-class — forall([s], s in top-space iff is-top-space(s))
  • totally-bounded — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) iff is-metric-space(s) and forall([r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies forsome([f], card(f) in nn and is-r-net(s, f, pts(s), r))))
  • trinum-succ — forall([n_ in nn], trinum(succ(n_)) == trinum(n_) + succ(n_))
  • trinum-zero — trinum(0) == 0
  • vector-space-class — forall([s], s in vector-space iff is-vector-space(s))
  • zen-limit — forall([x, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zen(x, lam) = choice({y_ in x: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, lam) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))
  • zen-succ — forall([x, alpha in ord], zen(x, succ_ord(alpha)) = choice({y_ in x: forall([c_], ord-le(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))
  • zen-zero — forall([x], zen(x, 0) = choice(x))
  • zero-ring-def — zero-ring = [{0}, vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian({0}, {0}), 0), vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian({0}, {0}), 0), vnb-lambda([p], {0}, 0), 0, 0]
  • zkept-limit — forall([phi, grd, porel, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zkept(phi, grd, porel, lam) = big-union(beta, ord-segment(lam), zkept(phi, grd, porel, beta)))
  • zkept-succ — forall([phi, grd, porel, alpha in ord], zkept(phi, grd, porel, succ_ord(alpha)) = union(zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha), keep-set(phi, grd, porel, zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha), alpha)))
  • zkept-zero — forall([phi, grd, porel], zkept(phi, grd, porel, 0) = empty-set)
  • zup-limit — forall([grd, porel, lam], limit-ord(lam) implies zup(grd, porel, lam) = choice({y_ in grd: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, lam) implies [zup(grd, porel, c_), y_] in porel)}))
  • zup-succ — forall([grd, porel, alpha in ord], zup(grd, porel, succ_ord(alpha)) = choice({y_ in grd: is-strictly-below(porel, zup(grd, porel, alpha), y_)}))
  • zup-zero — forall([grd, porel], zup(grd, porel, 0) = choice(grd))
  • zz-act-add-ring-additive-ag — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j + k, a) = (add(r))(zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j, a), zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a))))
  • zz-act-assoc-ring-additive-ag — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(r)], zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j * k, a) = zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), j, zz-act(ring-additive-ag(r), k, a))))

  • zz-bezout-set-membership — forall([a, b, z_], z_ in zz-bezout-set(a, b) iff z_ in zz and forsome([x_ in zz, y_ in zz], z_ = x_ * a + y_ * b))

  • prod-set-disjoint-union — forall([cm], is-comm-monoid(cm) implies forall([s1, s2, f], s1 in set and s2 in set and intersection(s1, s2) = empty-set and f in fun(union(s1, s2), carr(cm)) implies prod-set(cm, union(s1, s2), f) = (opr(cm))(prod-set(cm, s1, f), prod-set(cm, s2, f))))
  • prod-set-empty — forall([cm, f], prod-set(cm, empty-set, f) == iden(cm))

  • sum-ag-as-reduce — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(ag)), n], n in nn and 1 <= n implies sum-ag(ag, f, n) = reduce(opr(ag), f, n)))

  • sum-set-disjoint-union — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s1, s2, f], s1 in set and s2 in set and intersection(s1, s2) = empty-set and f in fun(union(s1, s2), carr(r)) implies sum-set(r, union(s1, s2), f) = (add(r))(sum-set(r, s1, f), sum-set(r, s2, f))))
